## Best date plans

### 2026-09-26 — Café Mars + Picture This: Animation Block Party

**Dinner, then Picture This: Animation Block Party.** Italian American at Café Mars. It is a 6-minute walk from the venue. Doors 7 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=272+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=702+Union+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://unionhallny.com/) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/picture-this-animation-block-party-tickets-1998619432524) · [Restaurant](https://cafemarsbk.com/)

### 2026-09-21 — Discussion: The Healing Power of Nature + Café Mars

**Discussion: The Healing Power of Nature, then a meal.** Italian American at Café Mars. It is 1.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=452+Flatbush+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=272+3rd+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/discussion-the-healing-power-of-nature/) · [Restaurant](https://cafemarsbk.com/)

**Timing:** 2-5pm conversation at Lefferts Historic House.
**Practical note:** Partly sunny near 72; the venue is in the park, so allow time to walk from transit.
**Why now:** One-day program on Monday afternoon in the window.
**On-sale watch:** Official listing says register today.

#### [Lincoln in the Bardo Immersive Cemetery Preview](https://www.green-wood.com/event/lincoln-in-the-bardo-immersive-cemetery-preview/)

6:00 PM · unusual · $15-$400; some tiers sold out · Green-Wood Cemetery · Green-Wood · Brooklyn · Score 46.0  
Death of Classical, Green-Wood, and the Metropolitan Opera stage an immersive cemetery preview of Missy Mazzoli and Royce Vavrek’s Lincoln in the Bardo.
**Timing:** Immersive experience 6-7:30pm; preview 7:30-8:30pm; return by 9pm.
**Practical note:** Hilly outdoor cemetery route; Friday night has a 50% shower chance and strong north winds. Grass and chair seats are listed sold out.
**Why now:** One-night preview precedes the opera’s October world premiere.
**Hot booking alert:** Official listing says grass and chair seats are sold out.

## Where to eat

Verified the strongest current dining leads against captured official pages and public editorial sources, keeping distinct, sourced offers/openings while correcting unsupported prices, stale freshness, duplicate cards, and ordinary-menu classifications.

Dining research: 2026-09-21T10:35:06+00:00

No Michelin-star claims were added. Eater’s Michelin-related language around Kirbee’s refers to the pitmasters’ prior Texas barbecue restaurants, not an award for Kirbee’s; that background should not appear as recognition for this NYC opening. Resy’s Hommali article mentions Baan Tepa as two-Michelin-starred background only, not an award for Hommali.

### $35 weekday lunch prix fixe at Greywind

Deals & happy hours — Greywind

Chef Dan Kluger’s Hudson Yards restaurant states it offers a $35 lunch prix fixe, a useful Midtown West/Hudson Yards weekday option near galleries and Penn/Moynihan.

Price: $35 lunch prix fixe  
Schedule: Monday-Friday 11:30 AM-2:30 PM  
Conditions: Official homepage and official menu page confirm the $35 prix fixe Monday-Friday 11:30 AM-2:30 PM; menu shows two courses with optional dessert +$5.50. Tax, tip, beverages, substitutions, sell-outs, and reservation requirements were not stated. Sources: https://www.greywindnyc.com/ and https://www.greywindnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.greywindnyc.com/)

### Friday $20 Happy Meal at Spygold

Deals & happy hours — Spygold

Greywind’s downstairs cocktail bar advertises a Friday $20 Happy Meal with a martini, slider, and fries, plus live music or a DJ.

Price: $20 for martini, slider, and fries  
Schedule: Fridays; Spygold hours listed as Friday-Saturday 5:00 PM-11:00 PM  
Conditions: Official Greywind homepage confirms Spygold’s Friday $20 “Happy Meal” with martini, slider, and fries plus live music or a DJ; the separate Spygold landing page/menu fetch did not show this offer, so keep the homepage-sourced offer and note exact serving window, tax/tip, substitutions, and sell-out limits are not stated. Sources: https://www.greywindnyc.com/ and https://www.spygoldnyc.com/  
[Source](https://www.greywindnyc.com/)

Special menus — The Mermaid Inn

The Mermaid Inn’s newly open Upper West Side location lists a distinct weekend Sunset Menu between brunch and dinner, useful for an off-peak seafood stop.

Schedule: Saturday-Sunday 2:30 PM-4:30 PM  
Conditions: Official Upper West Side location page confirms Sunset Menu Saturday-Sunday 2:30 PM-4:30 PM, plus Friday lunch and weekend brunch hours; captured text did not expose Sunset Menu items or prices. Source: https://www.themermaidnyc.com/the-mermaid-inn-upper-west-side/

### Creola five-course Black diaspora tasting menu

Top Chef alum Lana Lagomarsini’s intimate supper-club restaurant centers a seasonal five-course tasting menu inspired by the Black culinary diaspora, including dishes reported by Eater such as potlikker consommé and oxtail pâté en croûte.

Price: $125 tasting menu; $65 wine pairing reported  
Schedule: Wednesday-Saturday, two seatings nightly  
Conditions: Official site confirms supper-club tasting-menu format, Wednesday-Saturday service, two seatings nightly, and five seasonal courses; Eater reports $125 tasting menu and $65 wine pairing. ### The Mingle Games at Jaywalker with dumplings and cocktails

Pop-ups — Jaywalker at New York Distilling Co.
